rank.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"93:9","root":"ي ت م","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001692","branch_id":"B001","role":"The child cut off before maturity supplies a vulnerable present state that should not be frozen into a permanent identity."},{"source_ref":"93:9","root":"ق ه ر","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001266","branch_id":"B001","role":"Dominance from above supplies the danger that temporary stewardship becomes durable subordination."},{"source_ref":"93:4","root":"ء خ ر","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000019","branch_id":"B001","role":"Later-after-earlier supplies a trajectory beyond the orphan's present dependency."},{"source_ref":"93:4","root":"خ ي ر","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000452","branch_id":"B001","role":"Inclination toward beneficial good supplies the criterion by which present custodial action is judged."},{"source_ref":"93:4","root":"ء و ل","source_word_indices":["5"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000067","branch_id":"B002","role":"Return to outcome supplies the endpoint toward which intervention must be ordered."},{"source_ref":"93:4","root":"ء و ل","source_word_indices":["5"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001684","branch_id":"B003","role":"The non-dominant mapped image of taking charge supplies provisional administration rather than ownership."},{"source_ref":"93:4","root":"ء و ل","source_word_indices":["5"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001684","branch_id":"B008","role":"The non-dominant mapped image of priority supplies the beneficiary's claim to outrank the guardian's convenience."}],"structural_cues":["The explicit comparison between later and earlier creates temporal motion before the focus command addresses a status associated with childhood and interrupted protection."],"reader_inference":"The packet supplies temporal succession, beneficial outcome, administration, and priority; I infer that power over a dependent must aim at its own expiration in the dependent's agency. The live alternative is that 93:4 concerns only eschatological or personal consolation, while the split ء و ل→و ل ي branches are suggestive wordplay rather than governing semantics.","changed_reading":{"before":"Do not misuse authority while the orphan remains dependent.","after":"Exercise no authority that locks the orphan into dependence; any guardianship must be temporary, beneficiary-first, and ordered toward a better later condition."}},{"model_id":"d_giving_without_seizure_toward_assent","status":"revised","confidence":"medium","trigger_roots":["ع ط و","ر ب ب","ر ض و"],"mechanism":"The context reverses the direction of power: the superior gives rather than forcibly takes, reparative nurture rather than dominance structures the relation, and satisfaction can be mutual rather than imposed. Non-qahr thus acquires a consent-sensitive and agency-transferring shape.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"93:9","root":"ي ت م","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001692","branch_id":"B001","role":"The missing-protector condition supplies the unequal relation in which the recipient has limited bargaining power."},{"source_ref":"93:9","root":"ق ه ر","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001266","branch_id":"B001","role":"Forced taking without consent supplies the prohibited direction and manner of transfer."},{"source_ref":"93:5","root":"ع ط و","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001028","branch_id":"B002","role":"Handing over and giving reverse qahr's extractive flow by moving benefit toward the dependent."},{"source_ref":"93:5","root":"ر ب ب","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000532","branch_id":"B002","role":"Repair and upbringing make the transfer developmental rather than a display of patronal control."},{"source_ref":"93:5","root":"ر ض و","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000569","branch_id":"B003","role":"Mutual satisfaction supplies an interactional test: aid should not erase the weaker party's assent."}],"structural_cues":["The promised sequence 'He will give you, then you will be satisfied' places transfer and the recipient's resulting state before the later prohibition."],"reader_inference":"The packet supplies giving, reparative nurture, mutual satisfaction, and qahr as forced taking; I infer an ethical arrow from received gift to noncoercive provision. An alternative is that satisfaction describes only the addressee's future state and does not prescribe mutual assent in every act of care.","changed_reading":{"before":"Do not seize from or force the orphan.","after":"Reverse the flow of power: transfer benefit and growing agency toward the orphan in a manner responsive to their welfare and assent, rather than using aid itself as coercion."}},{"model_id":"d_received_shelter_becomes_active_repair","status":"strengthened","confidence":"strong","trigger_roots":["و ج د","ي ت م","ء و ي"],"mechanism":"The exact orphan condition reappears inside a found-and-sheltered sequence. This supplies a reversible pattern: one who received gathering and compassion when cut off must not merely avoid harm but actively close another orphan's protection gap.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"93:9","root":"ي ت م","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001692","branch_id":"B001","role":"The focus orphan's severance supplies the present protection gap to be answered."},{"source_ref":"93:9","root":"ق ه ر","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001266","branch_id":"B001","role":"Overpowering supplies the rejected use of the stronger party's position."},{"source_ref":"93:6","root":"و ج د","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001626","branch_id":"B001","role":"Finding and encountering supply recognition: vulnerability is first noticed rather than passed by."},{"source_ref":"93:6","root":"ي ت م","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001692","branch_id":"B001","role":"The repeated cut-off-child image identifies the addressee's remembered condition with the focus object's condition."},{"source_ref":"93:6","root":"ء و ي","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000070","branch_id":"B001","role":"Gathering into a sheltering side supplies the spatial reversal of isolation."},{"source_ref":"93:6","root":"ء و ي","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000070","branch_id":"B002","role":"Tender mercy toward one pitied supplies the affective manner of shelter, excluding humiliating patronage."}],"structural_cues":["The same ي ت م root occurs in the remembered second-person condition at 93:6 and the fronted object of the second-person command at 93:9.","The fa-sequence in 93:6 moves from finding to shelter, while the fa-sequence in 93:9 moves from identifying the orphan to prohibiting qahr."],"reader_inference":"The packet supplies exact root repetition and a found→sheltered sequence; I infer that remembered rescue is a template for present conduct and that the negative command carries a positive sheltering horizon. The alternative is a narrower gratitude reminder with no requirement beyond non-oppression.","changed_reading":{"before":"The verse asks for restraint toward an orphan.","after":"It asks the formerly sheltered addressee to relay shelter: notice the protection gap, gather the isolated person into safety, and let mercy rather than superiority define that inclusion."}},{"model_id":"d_correction_by_gentle_direction_not_humiliation","status":"new","confidence":"medium","trigger_roots":["و ج د","ض ل ل","ه د ي"],"mechanism":"A latent focus branch permits perceived heedlessness or shortcoming to enter the orphan image. The context answers lostness with gentle indication of a way, while a split branch keeps threat as a live rejected alternative. The result is a pedagogical reading of non-qahr.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"93:9","root":"ي ت م","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001692","branch_id":"B003","role":"Heedlessness or falling short supplies the perceived deficiency by which a guardian might rationalize harsh correction."},{"source_ref":"93:9","root":"ق ه ر","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001266","branch_id":"B001","role":"Humiliating compulsion supplies the prohibited educational method."},{"source_ref":"93:7","root":"و ج د","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001626","branch_id":"B001","role":"Finding supplies attentive recognition before any attempt to redirect."},{"source_ref":"93:7","root":"ض ل ل","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000913","branch_id":"B001","role":"Being off the intended way supplies the condition needing direction rather than punishment."},{"source_ref":"93:7","root":"ه د ي","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001583","branch_id":"B001","role":"Gentle indication toward road and truth supplies the constructive alternative to coercive correction."},{"source_ref":"93:7","root":"ه د ي","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001580","branch_id":"B010","role":"The non-dominant mapped image of threat and intimidation preserves the rejected alternative against which gentle guidance becomes vivid."}],"structural_cues":["The repeated 93:6-8 pattern finds a vulnerable condition and follows it with a remedy; 93:7 specifically pairs being off-course with guidance."],"reader_inference":"The packet supplies shortcoming, off-course condition, gentle direction, and a split-root threat image; I infer that perceived error never licenses qahr and that correction should guide without humiliation. The alternative is that ي ت م B003 and the ه د ي→ه د د split are too branch-distant to describe guardianship, so this remains a mechanism rather than a lexical gloss.","changed_reading":{"before":"Do not dominate a well-behaved dependent.","after":"Even when the orphan seems heedless or off-course, do not turn correction into humiliation or threat; first recognize, then indicate a way gently."}},{"model_id":"d_dependency_must_move_toward_sufficiency","status":"strengthened","confidence":"strong","trigger_roots":["و ج د","ع ي ل","غ ن ي"],"mechanism":"Poverty makes a person materially answerable to whoever controls provision. The context's found-poor→made-sufficient sequence therefore turns non-qahr into an economic trajectory: care should reduce the leverage of need instead of preserving it.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"93:9","root":"ي ت م","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001692","branch_id":"B001","role":"Loss of a protecting provider supplies the material as well as social exposure of the orphan."},{"source_ref":"93:9","root":"ق ه ر","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001266","branch_id":"B001","role":"Dominance and compelled taking supply the power that scarcity gives a provider over a dependent."},{"source_ref":"93:8","root":"و ج د","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001626","branch_id":"B001","role":"Finding supplies recognition of material vulnerability as a condition requiring response."},{"source_ref":"93:8","root":"ع ي ل","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001068","branch_id":"B001","role":"Poverty and want supply the dependency through which provision can become coercive leverage."},{"source_ref":"93:8","root":"غ ن ي","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001110","branch_id":"B002","role":"Sufficiency supplies the positive endpoint: enoughness that reduces dependence on another's arbitrary will."}],"structural_cues":["The third found-and-remedied sequence immediately precedes the three closing imperatives, linking remembered material repair to present treatment."],"reader_inference":"The packet supplies want, sufficiency, and superior force; I infer that economic dependence is one channel of qahr and that its remedy should diminish coercive leverage. A live alternative is that 93:8 records personal consolation without prescribing a full independence trajectory.","changed_reading":{"before":"Do not oppress the orphan while providing for them.","after":"Do not make provision a technology of control; answer material exposure in ways that move the orphan toward sufficiency and reduce the provider's leverage."}},{"model_id":"d_parallel_need_without_verbal_expulsion","status":"revised","confidence":"medium","trigger_roots":["س ء ل","ن ه ر"],"mechanism":"The exact parallel construction places structural vulnerability beside voiced need and places qahr beside rough rebuke. This makes speech one mechanism by which a stronger party can reproduce the orphan's social smallness; a split branch offers gentle drawing as an exploratory counter-motion.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"93:9","root":"ي ت م","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001692","branch_id":"B001","role":"The missing protector supplies unvoiced structural need."},{"source_ref":"93:9","root":"ق ه ر","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001266","branch_id":"B001","role":"Humbling from above supplies the broader social action that harsh speech can enact."},{"source_ref":"93:10","root":"س ء ل","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000661","branch_id":"B001","role":"Question and request supply need made audible, placing the response under an interactional test."},{"source_ref":"93:10","root":"س ء ل","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000736","branch_id":"B001","role":"The non-dominant mapped image of drawing out gently and quietly supplies an exploratory opposite to expelling the needy speaker."},{"source_ref":"93:10","root":"ن ه ر","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001559","branch_id":"B004","role":"Rebuking with coarse speech supplies the verbal form of making a vulnerable person recoil."}],"structural_cues":["93:9 and 93:10 repeat the frame فَأَمَّا/وَأَمَّا + vulnerable object + فَلَا + jussive verb, tightly pairing the two encounters."],"reader_inference":"The packet supplies exact syntactic parallelism, request, rough rebuke, and qahr as humiliation; I infer that tone can perform the same vertical relation as material coercion. The alternative is that the paired verbs deliberately retain separate scopes, with qahr no more than treatment and nahr no more than speech.","changed_reading":{"before":"Do not physically or institutionally overpower the orphan.","after":"Do not make need recoil through voice or posture: receive the structurally exposed orphan with the same non-expelling attention owed to an audible requester."}},{"model_id":"d_soft_care_as_visible_testimony","status":"new","confidence":"medium","trigger_roots":["ن ع م","ر ب ب","ح د ث"],"mechanism":"Softness, reparative nurture, and making something manifest turn the treatment of the orphan into evidence of how received care has been understood. Non-qahr is not only private abstention; it can make grace publicly legible without turning the orphan into an exhibit.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"93:9","root":"ي ت م","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001692","branch_id":"B001","role":"The orphan's protection gap supplies the concrete relation in which claimed gratitude is tested."},{"source_ref":"93:9","root":"ق ه ر","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001266","branch_id":"B001","role":"Humiliating dominance supplies conduct that would contradict any account of received care."},{"source_ref":"93:11","root":"ن ع م","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001525","branch_id":"B002","role":"Softness, ease, and well-being supply the texture that remembered blessing should reproduce in care."},{"source_ref":"93:11","root":"ر ب ب","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000532","branch_id":"B002","role":"Repair and upbringing supply the sustained practice through which softness becomes more than sentiment."},{"source_ref":"93:11","root":"ح د ث","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000299","branch_id":"B006","role":"Making a thing appear and become manifest supplies public legibility to the care relation."}],"structural_cues":["The orphan command is the first of three closing responses to the preceding remembered gifts, and the final response explicitly concerns making blessing known."],"reader_inference":"The packet supplies softness, nurture, manifestation, and a closing imperative sequence; I infer that treatment can narrate blessing performatively. The live alternative is that حَدِّثْ requires verbal recounting only, so conduct may illustrate but cannot replace speech; public narration must also avoid instrumentalizing the orphan.","changed_reading":{"before":"Non-oppression is a private minimum, while speaking of blessing is a separate verbal act.","after":"Soft, reparative treatment of the orphan is itself visible evidence of received care, though any public telling must preserve the orphan's dignity rather than display them."}}],"surprising_valid_outliers":[{"outlier_id":"o_heat_extraction_from_the_already_depleted","confidence":"exploratory","containment":"This is surprising because it activates the material ق ه ر image of fire making meat release its moisture, not the ordinary force sense of تَقْهَرْ. It remains anchored in the focus verb and is sharpened by contextual malnutrition and sufficiency. Downstream prose should label it a material analogy for extractive pressure, never a lexical translation or independent legal claim.","focus_anchor":"تَقْهَرْ at word 4 carries a branch in which heat seizes and alters flesh until its moisture runs, while ٱلْيَتِيمَ at word 2 supplies the unprotected subject exposed to such pressure.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"93:9","root":"ي ت م","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001692","branch_id":"B001","role":"Cut-off offspring supplies the person whose protective reserves are already reduced."},{"source_ref":"93:9","root":"ق ه ر","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001266","branch_id":"B002","role":"Flesh seized by fire until its juices run supplies a material model of pressure that extracts remaining substance while changing its subject."},{"source_ref":"93:8","root":"ع ي ل","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001068","branch_id":"B004","role":"Poor nourishment supplies the already-depleted condition that makes further extraction especially destructive."},{"source_ref":"93:8","root":"غ ن ي","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001110","branch_id":"B002","role":"Sufficiency supplies the reversal: restore usable substance rather than press until the remainder leaks away."}],"changed_reading":{"before":"Do not overpower the orphan.","after":"As a contained material analogy, do not subject an already depleted person to heat-like pressure that extracts their remaining money, labor, confession, or compliance; care should restore sufficiency."}},{"outlier_id":"o_preserve_peerless_form_without_grinding","confidence":"exploratory","containment":"This reading is branch-distant because it joins the orphan root's peerless-object image to ق ه ر's grinding-stone image and the closing context's polishing image. All three are packet-backed and the focus anchors are exact, but downstream prose should present the result as a cross-material model of formation, not as the direct denotation of the verse.","focus_anchor":"ٱلْيَتِيمَ at word 2 can image a solitary, peerless thing, and تَقْهَرْ at word 4 carries a hard-stone grinding branch.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"93:9","root":"ي ت م","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001692","branch_id":"B002","role":"Peerless singleness supplies a distinctive form that care should not erase merely because it lacks a matching peer."},{"source_ref":"93:9","root":"ق ه ر","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001266","branch_id":"B004","role":"A hard or smooth grinding stone supplies crushing standardization as the prohibited formative pressure."},{"source_ref":"93:11","root":"ح د ث","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000299","branch_id":"B007","role":"Polishing a blade or heart supplies a contrasting form of care that clarifies without pulverizing the underlying form."}],"changed_reading":{"before":"Do not crush a socially isolated person.","after":"As a contained formative analogy, do not grind the orphan's peerless form into conformity; cultivation may clarify and polish, but it must not pulverize distinctiveness."}},{"outlier_id":"o_do_not_drive_the_orphan_backward","confidence":"exploratory","containment":"This is surprising because it activates ق ه ر's branch of backward retreat through the adjacent qahqara form. It remains attached to the focus root and gains directional support from later-over-earlier and gentle guidance. It should be rendered as branch-level wordplay about trajectory, not as a claim that تَقْهَرْ literally means 'drive backward' here.","focus_anchor":"تَقْهَرْ at word 4 supplies the root inventory containing backward retreat, while the orphan at word 2 supplies a person whose interrupted protection can already delay forward movement.","activation_trace":[{"source_ref":"93:9","root":"ي ت م","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001692","branch_id":"B004","role":"Delayed motion and delayed care supply an already impeded trajectory."},{"source_ref":"93:9","root":"ق ه ر","source_word_indices":["4"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001266","branch_id":"B005","role":"Backward retreat supplies the outlier's directional image of authority reversing another's progress."},{"source_ref":"93:4","root":"ء خ ر","source_word_indices":["1"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000019","branch_id":"B001","role":"Later-after-earlier supplies a forward temporal horizon against which forced regression becomes visible."},{"source_ref":"93:4","root":"خ ي ر","source_word_indices":["2"],"mapped_root_id":"root_000452","branch_id":"B001","role":"Beneficial good supplies the quality required of forward movement."},{"source_ref":"93:7","root":"ه د ي","source_word_indices":["3"],"mapped_root_id":"root_001583","branch_id":"B001","role":"Gentle direction toward a way supplies the noncoercive forward alternative."}],"changed_reading":{"before":"Do not hold the orphan down in the present.","after":"As contained branch wordplay, do not drive an already delayed life backward; protect a gentle, beneficial path from interrupted dependence toward a later condition."}}],"summary":{"strongest_changes":["The exact 93:6 repetition changes a negative ban into a positive relay of shelter: notice, gather, and show mercy.","The 93:3 branch cluster recasts custody as entrusted repair and growth, making abandonment, heart-distance, and possessive rule co-forms of qahr.","The giving, satisfaction, poverty, and sufficiency sequences expose transfer and dependency as power channels: care should move benefit and agency toward the orphan without coercive leverage.","The 93:10 syntactic twin makes harsh response and humiliating dominance mutually illuminating, extending the focus command into voice and posture."],"what_was_special":"The focus branches opened a mechanism richer than generic kindness: a missing protector creates exploitable verticality, isolation can be turned into imposed lowliness, and delayed care can itself govern. Context then supplied positive reversals—gentle tempo, shelter, cultivation, guidance, assent, and sufficiency—while the material branches preserved live but contained images of heating, grinding, polishing, and backward motion.","remaining_uncertainties":["The ض ح و forbearance image and the privacy reading from night-covering may color manner without carrying the ethical mechanisms inferred from them.","The ء و ل→و ل ي and ه د ي→ه د د split-root activations are intentionally retained but should not be collapsed into the dominant lexical readings.","The positive duties of shelter, agency transfer, and sufficiency are strong contextual abductions, but the focus imperative itself is grammatically a prohibition rather than an explicit full program.","The material outliers are useful because they expose extraction and crushing, but they require explicit qualification as analogies rather than translations."]}}}},"v12_reader_walks":{"reader_a":{"activated_readings_md":"1. **Do not repeat the lost-protector wound.** Evidence: ي ت م B001 (orphaned offspring cut off from protector), ق ه ر B001 (overpowering from above that subdues), ء و ي B001/B002 in 93:6 (shelter; pity), ر ح م B001/B002 in 93:0 (tenderness; kinship bond). Structural cue: the command about the orphan directly answers the earlier recollection of being found orphaned and sheltered. Mechanism: the packet supplies prior rescue and present prohibition; I supply the transfer that receiving shelter prohibits becoming a crushing superior. Abductive move: the remembered cut-off state is meant to regulate power from above. Reading change: the command is not generic charity; it is a rule against weaponizing superiority over one whose protective bond is broken.\n\n2. **Do not delay kindness.** Evidence: ي ت م B004 (slowing/delay in motion or care; kindness delayed from orphan), ء خ ر B002 in 93:4 (deferring), ض ح و B001 in 93:1 (delaying to forenoon), ع ط و B002 in 93:5 (giving). Structural cue: earlier delayed goodness is reinterpreted positively, but here delay toward the orphan is dangerous. Mechanism: the packet supplies different kinds of delay; I supply the distinction that divine deferral ripens, while human delayed care toward the orphan can become harm. Abductive move: not every waiting is merciful; the vulnerable person's need changes the moral value of delay. Reading change: \"do not oppress\" includes do not let kindness arrive too late.\n\n3. **Do not grind/cook the vulnerable.** Evidence: ق ه ر B002 (meat seized by fire until juices run), ق ه ر B004 (hard/smooth grinding stone), ق ل ي B004 in 93:3 (frying/roasting), ر ح م B004 in 93:0 (womb ailment after birth). Structural cue: food/fire/body-pain branches appear around a command involving bodily-social vulnerability. Mechanism: the packet supplies violent material transformations; I supply the metaphor that oppression extracts, heats, or crushes the vulnerable until their inner resources run out. Abductive move: قهر's non-social branches sharpen the physical cruelty latent in domination. Reading change: the prohibition becomes visceral: do not process the orphan like matter under force.\n\n4. **Do not push the solitary backward.** Evidence: ي ت م B002 (isolated singleness), ق ه ر B005 (backward retreat), ض ل ل B003/B005 in 93:7 (lost thing/stray animal), ه د ي B001/B002 (guidance/course). Structural cue: after the lost one is guided forward, the orphan is not to be forced backward. Mechanism: the packet supplies backward-retreat and guidance-course branches; I supply the directional opposition. Abductive move: oppression reverses the movement of guidance by driving the vulnerable behind. Reading change: \"لا تقهر\" includes not disorienting or setting back the already isolated.","retrospective_surprises_md":"93:10 paired the orphan with the asker, so قهر could not remain only physical/social domination; it belongs to a broader reception ethic that also includes speech. 93:11 then made the surprise sharper: speaking of نعمة is only truthful if it does not become a form of superiority over the orphan.","turkish_prose_synthesis_md":null,"source_file":"quran-data/data/analysis/ayah-activation/v12-tr/s093/full_context_control/reader_s093_a_ayah_walk.md"}},"v12_reader_walks_wide":{"reader_a":{"activated_readings_md":"1. **Received protection must not be converted into downward force.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ي ت م:B001` is a child cut off from a protecting parent, and fixed `ق ه ر:B001` is overpowering from above that subdues and humiliates. **Structural cue:** 93:6 first presents the addressee in the same orphan condition and answers it with shelter; 93:9 repeats `ٱلْيَتِيمَ` but now changes the addressee's role from protected object to responsible actor. **Assigned role:** orphanhood marks exposed asymmetry; qahr names abuse of the now-superior position. **Abductive move:** I infer role reversal plus remembered shelter → duty to interrupt, rather than reproduce, the hierarchy of vulnerability. **Reading change:** the prohibition addresses the transmission of power across experience, not only a single forbidden act.\n\n2. **Do not compound the orphan's isolation.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ي ت م:B002` is isolated singleness, while fixed `ق ه ر:B001` includes humiliation and forced subjection. **Structural cue:** 93:6 had reversed isolation by gathering into shelter (`ء و ي:B001`); 93:9–11 then coordinate orphan, asker, and favor through three `أَمَّا` clauses. **Assigned role:** isolation is the social wound; humiliation would deepen it; grammatical coordination places the orphan within a shared field of claims and memory. **Abductive move:** I infer humiliating dominance → intensified exclusion, whereas coordinated address → recognition. **Reading change:** `فَلَا تَقْهَرْ` includes refusing acts that socially single the orphan out and press that isolation further.\n\n3. **The command also exposes delayed care as a form of failure.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ي ت م:B004` is slowness or delayed kindness toward the orphan; 93:5 postpones a gift through a future horizon, but 93:6 answers orphanhood with immediate-result `فَـَٔاوَىٰ`. **Structural cue:** purposeful futurity is set beside prompt shelter, then the orphan command. **Assigned role:** the delayed-kindness branch distinguishes legitimate temporal development from withholding care from a dependent. **Abductive move:** I infer that control over access and timing can enact domination; this extends `قَهْر` beyond overt force, and the packet does not state that extension directly. **Reading change:** the prohibition can diagnose coercive delay or neglect as well as active humiliation.","retrospective_surprises_md":"93:10 revealed that qahr belongs to a paired system of exclusion: 93:9 protects against force from above, while 93:10 protects against verbal driving-away. 93:11 then made the correction transmissive rather than merely restrictive, since speaking of favor after the two commands links remembered protection to present treatment of vulnerable others.","turkish_prose_synthesis_md":null,"source_file":"quran-data/data/analysis/ayah-activation/v12-tr-11ayah/s093/full_context_control/reader_s093_a_ayah_walk.md"}},"v12_cross_run_publication":{"source_file":"quran-data/data/analysis/ayah-activation/v12-cross-run/tr/93_ayah_findings_publication.json","protocol":"v12-cross-run-publication-v3","language":"tr","surah":93,"ayah_ref":"93:9","canonical_ayah_ref":"93:9","baseline":{"source_ref":"93:9","text":"Öyleyse yetimi ezme.","target_tokens":[["Öyleyse",["93:9:1"]],["yetimi",["93:9:2"]],["ezme",["93:9:3","93:9:4"]]]},"findings":[{"text":"Daha önce korunmuş olan kişinin rolü değişir: aldığı barınağı, koruyucusuz yetimin üzerinde aşağı doğru işleyen bir güce dönüştürmemelidir.","grade":"strong","anchors":[["93:9:2","root_001692",["B001"]],["93:9:4","root_001266",["B001"]]]},{"text":"Yetimin tek başına kalmışlığı, ezmeyi yalnız kaba güç değil, onu toplum içinde daha da yalnızlaştıran ve küçük düşüren bir baskı haline getirir.","grade":"strong","anchors":[["93:9:2","root_001692",["B002"]],["93:9:4","root_001266",["B001"]]]},{"text":"Yetimliğe iyiliği geciktirme boyutu, ihtiyaç sahibinin erişimini sürüncemede bırakmanın da baskıya dönüşebileceğini gösterir; - Prose: `_commentary/outputs/s093/93_9.prose.tr.md`
- Evidence surface: `_commentary/outputs/s093/93_9.evidence.tr.md`
- Findings index: `_commentary/outputs/s093/93_9.index.tr.md`
- Friction: `_commentary/outputs/s093/93_9.friction.tr.md`

1. **The prose** — per the output contract in `COMMENTARY_SPEC.md` §5 and the task document above: continuous, single voice, no provenance markers, no headers named after evidence layers.
2. **The evidence surface** — separate from the prose, addressable per phrase, per `PRINCIPLES.md` §12.
3. **The findings index** — one line per reading carried by the prose, under its bundle ref, with `[inference]` on the writer's own readings. Include each required `surprise:<id>` synthesis row and its `[supports-primary]` or `[shifts-primary]` relation.
4. A section headed exactly `=== PROMPT FRICTION ===` reporting honestly where the specification above was unclear, self-contradictory, underspecified, or impossible to follow, and any point where you had to invent a rule to proceed. Report this section every time, even if the run went cleanly — say so explicitly if you found nothing — and end it with the density audit required by the task document. This is how the specification gets improved.

## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision. Add no bulk without a distinct
reader payoff, but do not compress a significant finding for brevity.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response or
  confidence movement;
- if `v12_focus_trace_hermetic` is present, use its `baseline_models`,
  `context_deltas`, and `surprising_valid_outliers` as reconstructed
  before/after evidence. Do not call it a staged reveal transcript. Preserve
  surprising outliers when they remain anchored in this ayah, especially
  secondary split-root activations;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. They do not license recurrence, maturity, or a surah-wide
  channel name.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by related root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, developed through its final reader-visible result
   in proportion to its significance.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

Start each paragraph with reader meaning before grammar. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi
Allah'a verir; bunu fiille değil, sabit bir ad cümlesiyle yapar" over "Bu âyet,
tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik bir hüküm kurar."

Keep the prose anchored in this ayah's own wording without imposing a paragraph
or length quota. Give every significant finding enough space to make its
mechanism and reader payoff clear, even when it arrives through later context;
never compress it merely to keep the commentary short. Preserve every member of
the resonance set, and use a plain closing synthesis only when the ayah benefits
from one.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
confirm in friction that there was no live misconception, replacement risk, or
counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
